— *Why:* row 7.13 of `COORDINATION.md` spec — «с тегом `spec` с T166 — релиз и проверки без браузера и Go-тестов (lint, vuln, licenses, документы) в стадии `toolchain-core`» —, and row 7.18, whose last part this is (R59). The release of `module-kit` gains no time by it, since its `toolchain` adds nothing to the core, but its stages are now the template's, which a move to a tag takes byte for byte (R51), and a tool added later goes to the stage that keeps the release and the checks without tests small. "Binaries / build" builds the release's files to prove the binaries of each system equal (R41): in `toolchain` it takes the entry of the image that `go.yml` fills on every run, and the two stages hold the same files while `toolchain` adds nothing, which the new step holds.

— *Rejected:*
- "Binaries / build" in `toolchain-core`, as the body of T49 said, «в той же стадии, что `release.yml`» — the shared checks keep no entry of the core for a caller whose `toolchain` adds nothing to it (R47 .github), so the job would build the core on every pull request, or keep an entry of 382 MiB of its own (measurements 3.1 spec) for the same files; the guard gives the same proof while the stages are one, and fails once they are not;
- no guard — a tool added to `toolchain` that `release-files` came to need would pass here and fail the release on `main`; a guard of its own reading, not held to `go.yml` — a change of that reading in `.github` would leave the two disagreeing on whether the core is a stage of its own;
- the copy of «Toolchain image» with `STAGES: toolchain-core` and the key of `toolchain` — a file of one stage read as the other, which R47 .github keeps apart by the path and the key.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-16 amendments to R06, R07, R18, R41, R57, R58, R59 and R61. The hash of the Dockerfile changed, so the first runs miss the entries of the image and of the Go caches until a push to `main` saves them. The author rebuilds the container after the merge: `.devcontainer/` changed. The merge changes `release.yml` and `go.mod`, which are in the list `paths` (R18), and so gives the next release of the line 0.3, of the same code, its files built in `toolchain-core`. Epic E4 is done. — `.devcontainer/Dockerfile`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `codeql.yml`, `release.yml`, `scorecard.yml`; `go.mod`, `go.sum`, `spec/`; `.claude/rules/ci.md`; CLAUDE.md, README, RUN.md; [plan/E4.md](plan/E4.md); [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.1; [sources.md](sources.md), record 3.14; [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 3.4. — RUN.md, T49.

## The composite action `toolchain` of `.github` v0.1.32

**R64. CI of `module-kit` calls the shared checks of `.github` at v0.1.32, the release of row 2.5 of `COORDINATION.md` spec, by the SHA of its tag, 1704f78, in every `uses:` of `.github/workflows/`; "Binaries / build" takes the image of the stage `toolchain` and the caches of Go by the composite action of that release, `altnovatrail/.github/.github/actions/toolchain`, at the same SHA, called twice with the check `binaries` — before the build, and at the end of the job with `save: "true"` —, in place of its copy of the six steps of the job `arm64` of `go.yml`, and the guard of the copy goes. Dependabot moves the action with the shared workflows, in the group `shared-workflows`. A step «As in the pinned .github» holds what the job still has of `.github`'s: every reference to it at one SHA, the mount of the Go caches to the lines of the action's header, and the awk of «The stage of the release» to `go.yml` (R62). What the checks prove is the same. The task is T52, the one task of epic E6.** Row 3.11 of `COORDINATION.md` spec, by T172 spec and the author's answer to В36 (R63); R49 .github; R76 spec; R156 spec and the author's word of 2026-11-16 on the run of the whole «Порядок».
- **What v0.1.32 brings** against v0.1.31 (sources 3.15): the composite action `toolchain` (R49 .github), whose «Toolchain image» and «Go caches» are the scripts of the copy here but for a comment, with the same file, image, folder and keys; the job `arm64` of `go.yml` takes it itself, and nothing else in the workflows `module-kit` calls changed. No input changed. `release-verify` of `spec` v0.18.6 takes a signature of the shared `release.yml` at v0.1.32: its lower bound is v0.1.7.
- **What is changed here:**
  - `.github/workflows/` — the seven references, from `93c848f # v0.1.31` to `1704f78 # v0.1.32`, in `ci.yml`, `codeql.yml`, `release.yml` and `scorecard.yml`;
  - `ci.yml`, the job "Binaries / build" — the six steps «Toolchain image from the cache», «Toolchain image», «Toolchain image into the cache», «Go caches from the cache», «Go caches» and «Go caches into the cache» go, and the action takes their place: «Toolchain image and Go caches» with `check: binaries` before «Release files and samples», and «Go caches into the cache» with `check: binaries` and `save: "true"` after the upload of the artifact, where the save stood. The key of the Go caches is the same, `go-<OS>-<architecture>-binaries-…`, so the entries saved on `main` before serve on. «Release files and samples» mounts the folder and points Go into it by the lines the action's header names, as before;
  - the step «Cache steps as in the shared go.yml» becomes «As in the pinned .github», and holds no copy of steps. It fails the job, naming the reason, if the references to `altnovatrail/.github` in `.github/workflows/` are not at one SHA, so that the action is `go.yml`'s of the same release, with its file, image, folder and keys; if a line of the action's header at that SHA that names `/go-cache` — the three variables and the mount, which the action fixes for a caller's `docker run` — is not a line of «Release files and samples», indentation aside; and if the awk of «The stage of the release» is not that of «Toolchain stages» of the job `toolchain` of `go.yml` at that SHA, as R62 holds it;
  - `.github/dependabot.yml` — the comment of the group `shared-workflows` names the action; its pattern `altnovatrail/.github/*` matches the action's name in dependabot-core (sources 3.15), so the group needs no new pattern;
  - [.claude/rules/ci.md](../.claude/rules/ci.md) and [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.1 — the version, the action, the guard.
- **Checked:** `gh release verify v0.1.32 -R altnovatrail/.github` — "verified!"; actionlint 1.7.12 is clean on `.github/workflows/`; the script of «As in the pinned .github», run by hand, passes on this tree, and fails, each with its message, with `codeql.yml` at v0.1.31, with the mount `$RUNNER_TEMP/go-caches`, and with `"ENV"` for `"SHELL"` in the awk of «The stage of the release»; `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` is green. CI of the pull request runs the action in "Binaries / build", and "Binaries" is green on it.

— *Why:* row 3.11 of `COORDINATION.md` spec — «"Binaries / build" — на действии строки 2.5: копия шести шагов job `arm64` и страж, что держит её у закреплённого `go.yml` (R58, R61 module-kit), уходят, и pull request Dependabot группы `shared-workflows` проходит сам». Each move of `.github` that changed those steps turned "Binaries", a required check, red until a session edited the copy (Д21), and a merge by green checks waited for it. With the action, the steps are one file in `.github`, which the SHA that Dependabot raises brings.

— *Rejected:*
- dropping the guard whole — R62 rejected a reading of the stages of its own not held to `go.yml`: a change of that reading in `.github` would leave the two disagreeing on whether the core is a stage of its own; a mount that the action no longer fills would build cold and save an empty folder on `main` with "Binaries" green; and a reference to the action at another release than `go.yml` would read or save entries `go.yml` no longer names. A pull request of Dependabot that changes none of the three passes untouched; one that does fails, and takes the change by hand;
- the action with `check: arm64` or another check of `go.yml` — the entry of another job's builds (architecture 3.7 .github);
- the save right after «Release files and samples» — the job would save its caches before `binaries.sh` proved the binaries; the save stays where the copy had it, after the artifact;
- holding the mount of «Release files and samples» to the step `ci-test` of the job `arm64` of `go.yml`, as R61 — the action's header is the interface it names for a caller, and `go.yml`'s own recipes are its inner detail;
- the mount taken by the job from the action by an output — the action has none for it (sources 3.15).

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-17 amendments to R06, R07, R18, R41, R58, R61, R62 and R63; Д21 is closed and leaves `run/DEBT.md`. The merge changes `release.yml`, which is in the list `paths` (R18), and so gives the next release of the line 0.3, of the same code, signed by the shared `release.yml` v0.1.32. The keys of the image and of the Go caches did not change, so the entries of `main` serve the pull request. Epic E6 is in its archive, [plan/E6.md](plan/E6.md), with the measurement of its session in measurements 2.1. Whether a pull request of Dependabot that moves `.github` passes untouched, the next such pull request shows. — `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `codeql.yml`, `release.yml`, `scorecard.yml`; `.github/dependabot.yml`; `.claude/rules/ci.md`; RUN.md; [run/DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md); [plan/E6.md](plan/E6.md); [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.1; [sources.md](sources.md), record 3.15; [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 2.1. — RUN.md, T52.

— *Amendment 2026-11-18 (R68):* CI is on `.github` v0.1.33, by the SHA of its tag, f1c7e9e, and the action of that release is called as above; «As in the pinned .github» passes on it unchanged (R68).

## README without commands

**R67. README.md holds only what the component is and its place in NovaTrail: the header and its badges, what the library and the CLI do, and «Its place in NovaTrail» — its stage, that the format is spec's, who takes it and how, its card, architecture 3.7 spec, and a link to `docs/`. It holds no command and no block of code, no section «Releases», «Contributing» or «Documents», and no status, version, date or language. What it left and was nowhere else goes to [README.md](README.md) of `docs/`: the table of contents of `docs/` and of the plan, and the sections «Releases» and «Contributing», which CLAUDE.md, Documents, names; the answers of `.bestpractices.json` that pointed to README for how to take a release, contribute and run the checks point there. The task is T55, the one task of epic E9.** The author's word of 2026-11-17, while the task ran: «давай вообще уберём все команды из readme.md — этот подход уже устарел. никто из программистов не будет запускать команды вручную. всё будут делать агенты. Давай в readme оставим просто общее описание компонента и его место в архитектуре приложения и всё»; earlier the same day, «Contributing … засунь его в docs в README.md», the detail of build and run «в отдельном документе», and no nuance of languages; the text of the task the orchestrator gave (rule 6 of `COORDINATION.md` spec; R156 spec).
- **What is changed here:** README.md — the opening keeps the list of what the library does, which the answer `description_good` of `.bestpractices.json` quotes; «Its place in NovaTrail» takes what CLAUDE.md, The component, and the card say of its links, in a paragraph and a list of four; «Releases», «Contributing» and «Documents» go. `docs/README.md` holds «Releases» and «Contributing» as README held them, but for three edits: the binary is named `module-kit_<tag>_<os>_<arch>`, `.exe` on windows, as the release names it, and `gh release download` takes it with its bundle; `go get` takes `<tag>` in place of v0.2.1; the line others take is no longer «the line 0.2», which R52 left behind, but every release from v0.2.0, with a new line for each new version of the format (R16, R42, R52). Its opening lists `docs/`, RUN.md, `run/`, CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md and the submodule, as «Documents» of README did. `.bestpractices.json` — `interact`, `documentation_basics` and `test_invocation` name `docs/README.md`.
- **What holds the rule:** nothing in RUN.md, `run/`, AGENTS.md, `.claude/`, the justfile, CI or the tests reads a section of README; R65 named «Releases» and «Documents» of README, and gets the amendment below; the answers of `.bestpractices.json` linked `#contributing` of README. The answers on bestpractices.dev itself change when the author next takes the file there (R48 spec).

— *Why:* README is read by a person on GitHub, and nobody runs its commands by hand — agents do, from the documents they read; the detail went out of date there, as the line 0.2 and v0.2.1 had. GitHub links CODE_OF_CONDUCT, CONTRIBUTING and SECURITY of `.github` by itself.

— *Rejected:* a short «Releases» of two sentences and a block of commands — the author's first word of the day, which the second replaced while the task ran; the checks in a document of their own, `docs/build.md` — the list is four lines, and «Contributing» is where a contributor looks for them; the detail of releases in section 3.1 of [architecture.md](architecture.md) — it holds how CI builds a release, not how one takes it.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-17 amendment to R65. README.md, `docs/README.md`, CLAUDE.md and `.bestpractices.json` are in no list `paths` of the release (R18), so the merge gives no release. Epic E9 is in its archive, [plan/E9.md](plan/E9.md), with the measurement of its session in measurements 2.1. — README.md; [README.md](README.md); CLAUDE.md; `.bestpractices.json`; RUN.md, table 1; [plan/E9.md](plan/E9.md); [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 2.1.

— *Amendment 2026-11-18 (R68):* README.md has no heading below its title — what stood under «Its place in NovaTrail» stays, as paragraphs and the list — and no stage, by the second 2026-11-17 amendment to R21 spec; «Contributing» of [README.md](README.md) of `docs/` asks for issues in English, and `english_justification` of `.bestpractices.json` for issues and pull requests in English, by the 2026-11-17 amendment (T198) to R14 spec (R68).

## The Go caches by a prefix and the template's comments

**R68. CI calls the shared checks of `.github` at a release whose Go caches, on a miss of the exact key, come by its prefix without the hash of `go.sum`, their modules dropped and their builds kept, in every `uses:` of `.github/workflows/` and in the composite action `toolchain` (T75 .github; sources 3.16): `go mod verify` runs on an exact hit alone, and the builds of an entry by the prefix are held by the scope of `main`, as those of a hit. A move to a tag of `spec` takes `.devcontainer/` from the template but for a change of the template's comments alone, which waits for the next change of `.devcontainer/` beyond comments and comes with it (R51, R54).** Row 2.7 of `COORDINATION.md` spec, by the task text the orchestrator gave (R156 spec).

— *Why:* every move to a tag of `spec` changes `go.sum` here, and with the exact key alone the Go caches were cold after each. A change of the Dockerfile changes the key of the image of `toolchain` and of every Go cache and asks for a rebuild of the container, which a comment does not repay.

— *Rejected:* the template's comment of the Dockerfile taken with the move — a rebuild of the container and cold caches for a comment.

— *Where:* `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, the step «Toolchain image and Go caches»; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.1; `.claude/rules/ci.md`.

## The key of a task from paper

**R69. The key of a task from paper is a new package `paper`: the solver of a formula, with a parser of its own and exact rationals of `math/big`; the rules of an answer of AI; the key with its provenance; and the task's one item and the values read brought to the key, by which the package `observation` joins two readings and gives the verdict, as on a task of a module. It passes the vectors `paper/v1/` of `contracts.Vectors`.**

Decided while T229 spec was carried out, by its plan, which the author approved on 2026-11-19 (R162 spec; [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 16).

- **`Solve(formula)`** — `*paperv1.Number`, its value and the letter of an equation's unknown, or the `Reason` it is not taken. The reasons are those of the contracts text, the first that applies: the grammar, then the letters — two unknowns, an unknown in an expression, an equation with none —, then the operations as they are evaluated, each part before its operation and the left before the right — not linear, a division by zero, a part of more than 100 digits, the two sides of an equation brought together too —, then no root and every root, and last an answer longer than 16 characters. Each part is linear, a·x + b, its coefficients `*big.Rat`; the answer is an integer or p/q in lowest terms.
- **`Breach(answer)`** — the first rule an answer of AI breaks, or none: no kind, a value not in the notation of `number`, units empty or the same after the steps of units, an unknown not one letter, the count and length of options, options the same as a choice normalizes them, a key that is no option.
- **`Key(solved, ofAI)`** — one task's key, a `TaskKey` whose statement `api` sets (T235 spec): the solver's answer with `PROVENANCE_SOLVER` when there is one, or else AI's with `PROVENANCE_AI` when it breaks no rule, and with neither no answer and no provenance, as `key.proto` has it: a key with no answer says the task has none, its item unchecked at `api`, and `CAUSE_NO_KEY` when no task has one.
- **`Items(answer)`** — one item with one answer slot bound `anywhere`, its check `number` with the key and units, or `choice` with the options, the key and `notation: options`, and no trap values: what `observation.Validate`, `Join` and `Verdicts` take as they take a module's. An answer of no kind, a task with no key, gives no item: no check can be written of it, and its item is unchecked at the caller.
- **`Brought(answer, o)` and `BroughtValue`** — a copy of an observation whose values are brought to the key: a letter and `=` before the value are dropped when the letter, after steps 1 to 4, is the key's unknown, and the brackets round a label of a choice — `(C)`, `[C]`, `C)`. Any other value stays as it was read.
- **The vectors** — `internal/vectors/paper_test.go`: three kinds, `paper/v1/solver`, `paper/v1/answers` and `paper/v1/verdicts`, one per file, so that a file gone is a kind without a case. Each is read strictly; a value of the verdicts goes into the one answer slot, `null` as `UNREADABLE_BLANK`, through `Brought`, `Validate` and `Verdicts`. **Tests** of `paper` itself: `TestKey`, `TestItemsOfNoKey`, `TestBreachOrder`, `TestSolveBounds`, `TestJoinOfBroughtValues`, `TestBroughtCopies`, `TestParse`, and fuzz tests, the formula and the value read being foreign input: `FuzzSolve` — an answer or a reason, never both, and an answer is one `Breach` takes — and `FuzzBroughtValue`.

— *Why:*
- exact rationals: `1/3+1/7` is `10/21`, and a key a float rounds would give a correct answer "not yet" (R04 spec). The bound of 100 digits on the way keeps `(9^10)^10` from growing without end;
- a parser of its own, not the Starlark sandbox: the grammar is narrow, ASCII, and the solver must tell a linear equation from one that is not, which an evaluator of Starlark does not say; it needs no clock;
- the verdict through `observation`: the readers of a task from paper read as those of a module, and the join, the reasons of "unreadable" and the verdict are one code. Bringing the values first makes `x = 7` and `7` one value, so two readers who wrote them differently join;
- the item is built from the key, not kept: `api` keeps `paperv1.Key` on the attempt, and the verdict repeats from it and the observation.

— *Rejected:*
- `float64` — loses exactness;
- the solver in Starlark — its sandbox brings a clock and limits that a formula of 200 bytes does not need, and no answer of linearity;
- a check kind of its own for paper — `number` and `choice` already compare, and a kind is a new version of the format (R44 spec);
- bringing the values in `verdict` — that is the normalization of the [format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 15, and the letter of the unknown and the brackets of a label are rules of the [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 16, for a task from paper alone.

— *Where:* [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1 and 3.6; the package `paper/`.
